The cards are the same DOM as the table; the header row is clipped but stays for screen readers. From 768 px up nothing changes.
  - **Helper.** `ui/dom.js` has `tableBox(...)` and `table(headers, rows, { cards, caption, label, class })`, which builds `div.table-box > table.table`, sets `data-label` on each cell and, with `cards`, adds table ARIA roles. CSS is in `ui/css/responsive.css`.
  - **Other tables.** Admin repositories, Credentials, Audit, Dashboard, Models, Problems, Monitor, Operations, Watchers, the Work list and Users are wrapped in `.table-box` and scroll in their own box. `.table-box` now has `position: relative`, `max-width: 100%` and `min-width: 0`.
  - **Repository link.** In the Repositories list the repository is a link to its GitHub page when the address is a GitHub one (`https`, `git@` or `ssh`, with or without `.git`); other addresses stay plain text.
  - **Tests.** Unit cases in the UI tests and new browser specs in `tests/browser/tables.spec.ts`. `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` "Small screens" updated.
- Skill experience 3d — skills guide for administrators and skill authors (#440). New "Automatic skills" in `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` (what is automatic, what policy decides, writing a skill, budgets, trust, both pipelines, stop reasons and fixes), "Skills in your own flow" in `docs/FLOW_AUTHORING.md` and "The skill path" in `docs/DESIGN.md`; stale sentences corrected. New guide tests check the reason codes and fixes against the code. No behaviour change.
- Refinement 10 — checked with real situations, and documented (#83). Last part of the Refinement epic (#73).
  - **Next step.** New `ui/refinement-next.js`: the session page starts with a "What happens next" card, one sentence that says who takes the next step (you, the architect or nobody). It redraws only when the sentence changes. The "Ask the architect to look at the code" text is now one shared constant.
  - **Tests.** New scenario tests walk whole sessions with the fakes (a small clear idea, a vague idea, a big idea split into three stories published in order, an existing issue refined and updated), and rule tests prove the epic's rules (no unaccepted text published, architect steps cannot write, an implementation plan fails the check, users cannot see or publish another user's session, issue text does not steer the architect). New `tests/helpers/refinement-world.ts`, `tests/refinement-scenarios.test.ts`, `tests/refinement-rules.test.ts` and `tests/ui-refinement-next.test.ts`.
  - **Docs.** New "Refinement in short" (with a worked example) and "Refinement for admins" (Definition of Ready, the architect's model, where the cost shows) in chapter 12 of `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`. No flow format change, so `docs/FLOW_AUTHORING.md` is unchanged.
- Skill experience 3c — `issue-code-daily` uses the skills of the issue's plan (#439). The plan check of a coding run keeps what it found (`run.planCheck`: outcome word, stopped, changes since the plan; no ids or text). A carried request is checked again under `skills.unresolved` when the lock is made, as for an explicit flow, so a skill that cannot be used stops the run and resume checks again. The `skillView` has `planRecord` and the Skills card shows one plain sentence for it. The fake `gh` lists issue comments for `tools/plan-comment`; new `tests/skill-daily-flow.test.ts` plans and codes in separate runs and compares the lock with an `issue-gitflow` run. See "Skills from the plan to the coding run" in `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`.
- Refinement 9e-4 — the replaced original is closed as not planned (#406). Last part of #335.
  - **Order.** Creates, then the PATCH and comment per dependant, then one comment on the original saying the parts replace it and that it will be closed, then the original is read again and closed with reason `not_planned`.
  - **Not closed.** The original stays open, with a comment saying so, when the open issues were cut (more than 1,000), when a dependant ended as change by hand or check, or when it is a monitor story (a close would mute the finding). The state read from GitHub wins over `cut`: an original already closed is `closed: "other"`, or `not_planned` with `closedAt` when it was closed as not planned and the Foundry's comment is there.
  - **Races and retries.** Started to be built before the close: 409, no close, `replace` stays `"due"`. A retry after a failed comment or close writes one comment and one close; a close that landed but was not recorded sets `closedAt` without a call. A session write that fails after the close gives a 500 saying the issue was closed and that publishing again is safe. Sessions finished under 9e-3 (`closed: "open"`) stay done.
  - **API and UI.** `replaced` and the session view carry `closed` (`not_planned`, `other`, `open`) and `closedAt`; the plan's `replaces` has `staysOpen`. The plan paragraph, the `done` source line and the toast follow the three cases. The audit line also names the original when it is closed.
  - **Docs and tests.** The two publish rows in `src/server/permissions.ts` and the user guide ("Replacing a split issue", permission table). New `tests/refinement-publish-close-api.test.ts`; replace, retry, plan, dependants and UI tests updated.
- Refinement 9e-3c — the publish dialog and the session page show the replacement of a split issue (#419). Only `ui/refinement-publish.js`; no server change and no new route.
  - **Button.** **Publish** also shows when `source.replace` is `"due"`, also in a `published` session.
  - **Dialog.** When `plan.replaces` is set it says the issue is replaced by its parts (ready) or when every part is on GitHub (not ready), warns when `cut` (more than 1,000 open issues and pull requests), and lists the dependants with Before and After, or "Change by hand". The confirm button reads `Replace the issue` or `Create the issues and replace the original`, and is shown with an empty `willCreate` when `replaces.ready`. The toast names the replaced issue. All texts are set as text, never as HTML.
  - **Session page.** The source line shows waiting, due and done (with open or closed) before the update line; the page redraws when `replace`, `replacedBy` or `closed` change.
  - **Docs and tests.** "Replacing a split issue" under Publishing in `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`; new cases in `tests/ui-refinement-publish.test.ts`.
- Run detail 4d — event timeline in the Steps tab with collapsible detail (#394). New `ui/run-timeline.js` replaces `stepsView`; the Steps panel of `createTabs` is now `timeline.el` with `update: timeline.update`, and both run pages no longer redraw when `history.length` changes. No server change.
  - **Timeline.** Milestones in time order: run started; each finished step (passed or failed, visit number, duration); "Waiting for approval" with its message; "Running <step>"; each answer given; run finished with its status. Sub-steps (a `parent`) are indented under their parent. Each label is the step description from `flowDef`, else the step id, never the id alone. Entries with no time keep history order.
  - **Detail.** An administrator opens a step for its error under "Details" and its output or transcript, loaded on first open with the paged transcript view; tool calls stay closed until clicked. A user sees plain rows with the one-sentence error and no output, transcript request, cost, tokens, agent or model (the `"output" in s` check, so a user-shaped record never asks for a transcript).
  - **Live.** `update(s)` diffs by `key` (`step:<index>` for history), appends new nodes and replaces only the "current" and "end" rows, so open entries, loaded transcripts, scroll and focus stay. A failed run opens the step `failedStepIndex` picks once; **Show the failed step** selects the Steps tab and scrolls to it. Empty: "No steps finished yet." while running, "No steps recorded." when finished. A step body is built only when opened, so 500 steps draw without building any.
  - **Code.** `milestones(s)` (pure), `createTimeline({ runId, admin, a })` → `{ el, update, open }`. `stepEntry` and `failedStepIndex` moved there and are re-exported from `ui/runs.js`; `stepsView` is removed.
  - **Docs and tests.** "The run page" and the Steps list in `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`, line numbers in `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md`. New `tests/ui-run-timeline.test.ts`; step cases in `tests/ui-next.test.ts` and `tests/ui-user-runs.test.ts` are updated.
- Skill safety 1b — the `skills.tools` policy and the pure tool check (#429). No behaviour change yet: nothing calls the new module, so no run behaves differently.
  - **Config.** New `skills.tools` with `shell` (default `true`), `network` (`false`), `filesystem` (`"read"` or `"write"`, default `"write"`; `"none"` is refused) and `missing` (`"stop"` or `"degrade"`, default `"stop"`). An unknown key is refused. It only decides which skills may be loaded; it does not limit what a session can do.
  - **Tool check.** New `src/skills/tool-policy.ts`, pure (types only from the flow and skill schemas). `sessionTools` and `flowTools` give the capabilities of a Claude step or a whole flow (from permission mode, approved rules and sandbox, each with a source; `partial` when the flow has a `flow` step). `skillNeeds` gives what a skill asks for. `checkSkillTools` gives `continue`, `degrade` or `stop` with the codes `tool-not-approved`, `tool-not-in-flow`, `tool-not-in-step` and `connector-unavailable`. A named connector is always `connector-unavailable`. A mandatory skill with a missing need always stops; under `degrade` other skills are left out with their dependants and orphaned dependencies. `tool-not-in-step` never stops a run. `assessSkillTools` and `sessionToolGrant` build on it.
  - **Skill request.** The extra keys `tools`, `allowed_tools`, `connectors` and `tool_profile` are refused at top level and inside a skill item, in both parsers.
- UI redesign workspace 5a — Start work is a guided form: required first, the rest under "More options" (#354). `ui/user/start.js`, `ui/css/pages/start.css`. No server change; the request body `{ flow, task, vars }` is unchanged.
  - **Flow choice.** With one published flow the page shows no flow choice and no flow name. With two or more, a picker ("What do you want to start?") shows only each flow's title and description. The numbered legends are gone.
  - **Required first.** The repository (when the flow asks for one), the task and every `required: true` input come first, each marked "(required)". On Task and on an optional repository the mark is only a mark: validation is unchanged.
  - **More options.** Optional inputs and fixed values are in a closed `<details class="start-more">` whose summary gives the count (for example "More options: 2 optional fields, 1 fixed value"). It is not drawn when empty, opens when the server refuses the start, and closes when you change flow. Flow radios are disabled while a start is in flight.
  - **Repository state.** The repository row shows "Connected", "Not tested yet" or "Failed". A "Failed" repository shows a note with a link to `#/repos`.
  - **Code.** New pure exports `splitFields(flow) → { required, optional, fixed }` and `moreSummary`, and constants `MORE_OPTIONS`, `REPO_FAILED_NOTE` and `PICK_FLOW`. `startProblem` and `startBody` are unchanged; `homeHash` no longer exists. Six new `.start-form` rules, also in `tests/fixtures/ui-style-baseline.css`.
  - **Docs and tests.** "Start work as an admin" in `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`. `tests/ui-user-start.test.ts` is updated and extended; three titles in `tests/ui-user-start-states.test.ts` no longer say "step".

- Administration 8e — Overview and detail for providers (#366). A provider opens at `#/models/:name`; the Models list stays and keeps routing. No new API route and no server change.
  - **List.** Each provider name links to its page; the status is a ready or not-ready pill. The **Remove** button is no longer in the list.
  - **Detail.** `renderModelDetail(main, name)` (`ui/models.js`) shows the status, models and agents, the settings form (`PUT /api/config` with the same body as before), a **Try a model** box that starts on this provider, and **Use in routing** (default model, rules and fallbacks that use it, with a link to the Models page). **Remove provider** is in a **Danger** group, asks first, and returns to the list. A built-in provider is saved as an override. An unknown name shows "This provider is not set up." with a link back; a 403 shows `permissionState`, other failures `errorState` with Retry.
  - **Pages.** `ui/ia.js` adds page `model` (`#/models/:name`, `nav: "detail"`, admin only; a user is redirected to `#/runs`); crumbs are Administration › Models › name. `ui/app.js` routes `#/models/<name>` to it. `refreshModelLists` keeps its signature. New exported helper `providerOfSpec(spec, names)`.
  - **Test box.** The last test result belongs to the page it was started on, so the list and each provider show only their own.
  - **Docs and tests.** `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("Providers and local models"), `docs/ui-redesign/inventory.md` and `prototypes.md`. New `tests/ui-models.test.ts`; `tests/ui-ia.test.ts`, `tests/ui-routers.test.ts`, `tests/ui-a11y-admin.test.ts` and `tests/ui-models-states.test.ts` are extended.
- Skill experience 3b — planners get the skill catalogue (#438). The `plan` and `revise_plan` steps of `issue-gitflow` and `issue-plan` use `skills: catalog`: the catalogue of pinned, approved or built-in skills goes before their prompt and no skill block is attached. The new `src/skills/run-catalogue.ts` builds it; a skill is offered only when the resolver would select it for the coder. The run log has one `skill catalogue:` line per planner step, with a `hidden (not pinned)` count. The step record has a new optional `skillCatalogue` (`entries`, `omitted`, `estimatedTokens`, `hidden`; counts only). A failure to build the catalogue never stops planning. `renderSkillCatalogue` now escapes `<` in its JSON lines. **Upgrade note:** skills are now active for existing installs, and a Claude session whose lock holds skills runs without the personal Claude setup (MCP servers, plugins, hooks, settings); turn it off with `skills.builtin: false` and no roots, or `skills: {mode: off}` in a custom flow. **Planner tools:** `plan` and `revise_plan` of the two flows now have only Read, Glob and Grep (no scoped Bash tools). The test fakes gain `REQUEST_SKILLS` / `REQUEST_CATALOGUE_SKILLS` prompt lines.
- Administration 8d — Overview and detail for watchers and problems (#365). No new API route.
  - **Watchers.** `#/watchers` is a compact list grouped by repository: id, state mark, what it does, last successful check and a problem line. The id links to the new `#/watchers/:id` page with next steps, notes, error details, recent activity and, for the monitor, the bug-story switch and findings. **Check now**, **Edit** and **Enable**/**Disable** are routine actions; **Delete** is apart in a **Danger** group. "Gone" and "from config.yaml" watchers keep their restricted buttons. List rows have no Delete button.
  - **Problems.** A finding has the address `#/problems/:id` (`renderProblemDetail` in `ui/problems.js`, loads `GET /api/monitor/findings/:id`) with the evidence, bug stories, runs and its actions. **Mute** and **This is not a problem** are grouped apart from **Make a story now** and **Try again**. An unknown id shows the server's sentence with a link back. The list keeps the monitor sentence, the switch, mutes and detectors, and has no Mute button.
  - **Pages and crumbs.** `ui/ia.js` has pages `watcher` and `problem` (`nav: "detail"`); crumbs are `Administration › Watchers › <id>` and `Administration › Problems › <id>`. Both addresses redirect a user to `#/runs`. `ui/app.js` routes `watchers` and `problems` with and without an id.
  - **Code.** The watcher code moved from `ui/admin.js` to new `ui/watchers.js` (`renderWatchers`, `renderWatcherDetail`). `ui/admin.js` re-exports `lastOkText`, `watcherNotes`, `storiesLine`, `storiesRow`, `watcherStateMark`, `describeWatcher`, `watcherGroups` and `monitorEntry`, so other modules import as before.
  - **Docs and tests.** "The Watchers page" and "The Problems page" in `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`. `tests/ui-watchers.test.ts`, `tests/ui-problems.test.ts` and `tests/ui-ia.test.ts` are extended; other UI tests are updated for the move.
- Accessibility 6 — accessibility statement, manual scripts and the critical-journey gate (#390).
  - **Statement.** New `docs/ACCESSIBILITY.md` (target WCAG 2.2 AA, what is checked and by which test, known limits, how to report) and a static page `ui/accessibility.html` with the same wording. The account menu of both displays (`ui/index.html`, `ui/user/index.html`) has an **Accessibility** link; the menu is now `.account-menu` in `ui/css/pages/shell.css`. Reports go to the `accessibility` label at `bugs.url` of `package.json`. The page is a static file served before sign-in and holds no data, so it needs no rule in `permissions.ts`.
  - **Scripts.** New `docs/ACCESSIBILITY_SCRIPTS.md`: keyboard and screen-reader steps with expected results and an empty results table for each of nine journeys. Nothing was run in a browser or with a screen reader.
  - **Gate.** New `tests/ui-a11y-journeys.test.ts` draws the screens of each journey for both roles and fails on a `serious` violation from `audit()`. It checks that the page and the doc state the same standard and address, and that every journey in the scripts has a test. New surfaces must add their screen there. `tests/net.test.ts`, `tests/ui-gallery-server.test.ts` and the CSS baseline are updated for the page and the menu.
  - **Docs.** "Accessibility" in `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`.
- Administration 8c — Overview and detail for users and audit records (#364). No new API route.
  - **Users list.** `#/users` is a kit table with name, e-mail, role, status, last sign-in, runs and limits. It has no actions column and no row buttons; the name links to `#/users/<id>`.
  - **Account page.** New `#/users/:id` (`ui/user-detail.js`, `renderUserDetail`) shows the account's facts and three groups: **Actions** (Edit, Limits, App repositories, New link, View as user), **Security** (Reset password, Unlock, Block or Unblock) and **Danger** (Delete). Which buttons show follows `actionsFor`. The dialogs, API calls and follow-ups are the same as the old row buttons (a reload, a toast, sign-out for your own account); after deleting another account the page goes to `#/users`. An unknown id shows "This account does not exist." with a link back to Users. Crumbs: `Administration › Users › <name>`.
  - **Audit.** Each line has a **Details**/**Hide** button that opens a row with time, who (with id), action, target (with id), detail and result. It makes no API call. An account id links to `#/users/<id>`; a deleted account's link lands on the "does not exist" page. Opened lines close when a filter reloads the list. Filters, the "more" line and Export CSV are unchanged.
  - **Code.** `ui/ia.js` has page `user` (`#/users/:id`, parent `users`, `nav: "detail"`). `ui/shell.js` `namePage(name)` replaces the id in the last crumb, the page title and the tab title with the account's name. `ui/users.js` exports `DIALOGS`, `LABELS`, `browserPage`, `loadUsers` and `limitsCell`. `ui/app.js` routes the new address; a late answer no longer overwrites the page you moved on to.
  - **Docs and tests.** `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` (Users page, Audit page), `docs/ui-redesign/inventory.md`, `prototypes.md` and `dialogs.md`. New `tests/ui-user-detail.test.ts`; `tests/ui-users.test.ts`, `ui-users-states`, `ui-audit`, `ui-ia`, `ui-shell`, `ui-a11y-admin`, `ui-routers` and `tests/helpers/flow-app.ts` are updated. `tests/browser/visual-matrix.ts` masks `table.scf-table`; the Users screenshot baselines need regenerating.

- UI quality 1b — documented breakpoints, touch-sized targets, bottom-sheet dialogs and wrapping page actions (#368).
  - **Breakpoints.** compact below 768 px, medium 768–1023 px, wide 1024 px and up; 1100 px stays "two panes side by side". The compact rule moved from `max-width: 760px` to `767px` in all CSS and in `initShell`, so 768 px is the first width with the sidebar. New `ui/viewport.js` exports `BREAKPOINTS`, `STACKED_MAX`, `queryFor` and `mediaFor`; `initShell` and `renderWork` take their default `media` from it. `docs/DESIGN.md` has the table.
  - **Touch.** New tokens `--size-touch` (44 px) and `--size-touch-min` (24 px). On coarse pointers buttons, `.btn` links, inputs, selects, `summary`, navigation links, table row actions and the dialog close button are at least 44 × 44 px with 8 px between neighbours; on fine pointers at least 24 × 24 px. `.scf-btn` gets the 44 px minimum too. The old coarse-pointer block in `pages/shell.css` is gone.
  - **Sheets and wrapping.** Below 768 px `.modal` and `.scf-dialog` are bottom sheets (full width, at most 90 % high, scrolling inside, sticky head with the close button). `.toolbar` wraps below the title; `.log`, `pre.diff` and long text scroll or wrap in their own box. The Users table is wrapped in `.table-box`, which fixes its sideways scroll at 768 px.
  - **Code.** New `ui/css/responsive.css` (not `ui/responsive.css`), imported last by `ui/style.css`; no extra `<link>`. `/viewport.js` is served and the CSP allows it.
  - **Tests.** New `tests/ui-viewport.test.ts` (every CSS and JS width is a documented value, tokens, coarse and sheet rules) and `tests/browser/responsive.spec.ts` (`npm run test:ui`: no sideways scroll, 44 px targets with a touch profile, sheet at 360 px and centred dialog at 1024 px). The two `test.fixme` cases in `navigation.spec.ts` are removed. `tests/browser/helpers.ts` gets the `touch` option and `overflowing`.
  - **Docs.** `docs/DESIGN.md` ("Breakpoints and touch targets", browser tests), `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("Small screens"; 760 px changed to 768 px).
- Skill runtime 4 — load locked skills into Codex and local-model sessions (#179, #422).
  - **Same block.** A Codex step of a run whose lock holds skills gets the same `<foundry-skills>` block as a Claude step, after `</instructions>` and before the task. This holds for local models (`codex:ollama:m`, `ollama:m`) and boxed steps too, with no flag and no note. `skillSession(engine, deps, role, block)` no longer takes an `agent`; every agent gets the payload.
  - **Record.** The step record of a Codex step holds `skills` (`loaded`, `omitted`, `bytes`, `estimatedTokens`, as for Claude).
  - **Isolation.** A lock with skills turns Codex isolation on even with `isolate_agents: false` (the modes of #421). Nothing is written into the workspace. Runs without a lock are unchanged.
  - **Budget refusal.** A mandatory skill that does not fit `skills.selection.max_tokens` now stops a Codex step too, before Codex starts, with the same reason as for Claude.
  - **Review steps on Codex.** A review step on Codex gets the coder skills of the run's lock, as Claude does. A block for a draft plan (no lock yet) does not isolate a Codex session.
  - **Every session.** Codex never reuses the block: a resumed, retried or fallback Codex session gets the full block again. The log says "the full block is sent again".
  - **Old Codex CLI.** A step with a locked skill block is refused on a Codex CLI that has no `--ignore-user-config` (`unsupported` mode): `skill selection is blocked: this Codex CLI has no --ignore-user-config …`. Update the CLI, or use a local model or `CODEX_API_KEY`. Without a lock, such a CLI still runs as before.
- Skill safety 1a — skill packages may not hold credentials or live endpoints, and may name connectors (#428).
  - **Connectors.** `skill.yaml` accepts `connectors`: a list of slugs (at most 16, no duplicates, default `[]`). A package that names connectors needs `risk: medium` or `high`. `connectors` is in the parsed package, sorted. Nothing enforces it yet. Existing `skill.yaml` files parse as before and digests do not change.
  - **Scan.** `parseSkillPackage` scans every file name, the folder name and every file's content (`SKILL.md`, `skill.yaml`, `REVIEW.md` and all folder files). It runs before any file is parsed. There are four messages: `is a credential file by its name; a skill must not hold credentials`, `holds what looks like a credential or a live endpoint (<rule>); use a placeholder`, `a file name looks like a credential (<rule>)` and `the folder name looks like a credential (<rule>)`. They name the rule, never the value. On a hit only the scan's findings are shown, so no later message can repeat the value.
  - **Folder name.** `SkillPackageError` prints a credential-looking source as `(package)`, and the registry shows such a folder as `(name not shown)` in the skill problems.
  - **Code.** New `src/skills/credential-scan.ts` (`credentialProblem`, `riskyFileName`). Its 14 credential rules are copies of `tools/secret-scan`; `tests/skill-credential-scan.test.ts` fails when the two drift apart.
  - **Behaviour change.** A package that trips the scan no longer loads, also when it was pinned. It shows as a skill problem in health and `scf skills`, and a run that locked it stops with `skill integrity: … is missing`. Fix: replace the value with a placeholder or an example host, give the package a new version and pin again.
- UI states 4c — Admin run page: dialogs for Cancel, Re-run, Approve and Reject, and "needs you" on the page (#435). The four native `confirm(` and `prompt(` calls in `ui/runs.js` are gone.
  - **Dialogs.** New `ui/run-dialogs.js` holds `decisionDialog` (moved unchanged; `ui/user/runs.js` re-exports it) and `adminRunActions`. Cancel and "Retry from step…" use the shared `confirmDialog`; the Re-run select goes back to "Retry from step…" after confirm or decline. Approve and Reject use `decisionDialog`: a note field, and a server refusal shows inside the dialog with the note kept. A refused Cancel, Re-run or Resume shows the server's sentence as an alert line on the page. Success toasts stay; the `closed` check stays.
  - **Redraw.** The head of the admin run page is not redrawn while a dialog is open (`dialogOpen()` next to the field-focus hold); the newest update is drawn when it closes. A page-less 403, 404 or queued answer waits too.
  - **Needs you.** `nextBlock` (`ui/next.js`) also shows `next.text` when `next.who` is "You", in both displays, from the server record. The read-only preview shows it with no action buttons.
  - **Docs and tests.** Four rows moved from "Native dialogs" to "Dialogs" in `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md` (counts fixed), `inventory.md`, Runs row in `states.md`. New cases in `tests/ui-runs-detail-states.test.ts`, `tests/ui-user-run-states.test.ts` and `tests/ui-next.test.ts`.

- Administration 8b — Operations overview (#363). New page `#/operations` (**Overview**), the landing page of Administration: Health, Problems, Providers, Queue, Watchers, Limits and Recent audit on one page, each with a link to its own page. Read only, except **Cancel run** in the Health card; no new API route. A failed call shows "Not available" in the cards and tiles that depend on it.
- UI states 4b — Run page: skeleton, not found, no permission, stream banner and Retry (#434). `renderRunDetail` (`ui/runs.js`) and `renderMyRun` (`ui/user/runs.js`) draw every first-load state and keep what they already showed.
  - **States.** A detail skeleton (`loadingState(…, { shape: "detail" })`) shows until the first summary arrives. A 404 shows `NOT_FOUND` with a link back, a 403 shows `permissionState` with a link back, and any other first-load failure shows `errorState` with Retry. The admin page now calls `api.run(runId)` once at the start and again when the stream closes, to tell 404 and 403 from a lost connection. A queued run has no record yet, so on a 404 the admin page also asks `api.queue()`: a run found there shows a small "Queued run" head with no action buttons, and the full page replaces it on the first stream update. No server change.
  - **Stream.** "Lost connection to the run stream" is one `banner("error", …)` with **Reconnect** (`data-focus="stream-reconnect"`) instead of a toast. It shows only when the stream is closed for good (`readyState` 2), not while the browser retries, and repeated `onerror` does not add a second. The last head, steps and log stay visible. Reconnect closes the old `EventSource`, opens a new one with the same handlers, clears the log (the stream replays it) and removes the banner on the first event; an ended preview (`api.events` throws) is caught. The cleanup closes the current stream, also after a Reconnect. The read-only preview shows no action buttons in any state, and a closed stream there still does the GET check that ends the view.
  - **Retry.** A transcript or diff load failure shows an inline `errorState` with Retry (`step-<i>-retry`, `diff-retry`); Retry asks again and draws the content on success.
  - **Code.** `ui/run-states.js` gets `NOT_FOUND`, `NO_ACCESS`, `STREAM_LOST`, `isRun`, `runLoadKind`, `runLoadState`, `loadInto` (inline loading, error and Retry) and `runStream` (stream and banner). `createLog` in `ui/run-output.js` gets `clear()`. Older runs without `flowDef` still draw. The admin hold logic and the user page `runSeq`/`queueSeq` guards are kept.
  - **Docs and tests.** Runs row in `docs/ui-redesign/states.md`, two lines in `docs/ui-redesign/inventory.md`, line references in `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md`, "The run page" in `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`. New `tests/ui-runs-detail-states.test.ts` and `tests/ui-user-run-states.test.ts`; the toast case in `tests/ui-user-runs.test.ts` is now the banner case; `tests/ui-run-output.test.ts`, `tests/ui-a11y-live.test.ts`, `tests/ui-next.test.ts` and `tests/ui-redesign-prototype.test.ts` are updated.

- Flow editor 4 — Block library: find, preview and insert (#361). All in `ui/library.js`; the `/api/blocks` routes are unchanged.
  - **Insert from library.** The dialog has a search box, a category filter and a scope mark on each block. Picking a block opens a preview before anything changes: the steps (id and type), the variables it adds, and the ids that will be renamed. **Insert** adds it; **Back** returns to the list. An invalid block is listed with its error and cannot be picked. After Insert the first inserted step is selected, and the toast names the block and any renames as before.
  - **Library page.** `#/library` has the same search and category filter, a count of shown blocks ("3 of 12 blocks"), and an empty state with **Clear filters** when nothing matches. YAML view and delete for non-built-in blocks are unchanged, and so is **Save as block**.
  - **Code.** New pure `filterBlocks(blocks, { q, category })` (shared by the dialog and `renderLibrary`), `countText` and `previewInsert(flow, block)` returning `{ steps, vars, renamed }`; it shares its rename logic with `insertBlock`, so the preview matches the insert. `pickBlock(flow)` takes the flow (`onLibrary` in `ui/flow-page.js` passes it) and resolves with the block only after Insert.
  - **Block check.** `BlockSchema` (`src/flow/blocks.ts`) now also rejects duplicate step ids and reserved step ids, so such a block is listed as invalid instead of failing on insert.
  - **Docs and tests.** `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md` (Insert from library row), `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("The block library"). New `tests/ui-library.test.ts`; `tests/blocks.test.ts` and `tests/ui-flow-page.test.ts` are extended.
- Flow editor 3 — step list and a focused inspector with progressive disclosure (#360). The visual editor shows a structure list ("Flow settings", then one row per step with number, id, type icon, a one-line summary and marks for "only via jumps" and "has a problem") and an inspector that draws only the selected item. `renderEditor(flow, ctx)` keeps its signature; `ctx.selected` is `"settings"` (default) or a step index. The YAML written for an unchanged flow is the same as before; `cleanFlow`, `setPublishMode`, `movePublishVar`, `publishPanel`, `renameStep` and `uniqueId` are unchanged.
  - **Step inspector.** Shows the essentials by type: agent (prompt, model), shell (command), approval (question), parallel (step picker), sub-flow (flow and variables). No control of another type appears ("Needs repository access" only on a shell step). Other options are in closed groups that open by themselves when they hold a value: Routing, Limits, Sandbox & access, Model & agent settings. An approval step has no model, sandbox or tools group; a parallel or sub-flow step shows pass/fail patterns only when a value is set. A step of an unknown type shows the "edit it in the YAML tab" message. Flow settings use the same groups: basics, Defaults for agent steps, Safety, Variables, Publish to users.
  - **Actions.** Add step, move up/down, duplicate, delete, Save as block and rename (with reference update) work from the list and the inspector header. Selecting a node in the overview or a problem opens that step, focuses the field and opens a closed group that holds it. At 1100 px and below the list is above the inspector.
  - **Code.** `ui/step-types.js`: `STEP_TYPES` gets `groups` per type; `stepBody` is split into `stepMain` and `stepGroups` (returns `[{ id, title, open, nodes }]`); `stepBody` and `stepAdvanced` stay as thin wrappers. `ui/graph.js` exports `subtitle()` for the row summary. `ui/flow-page.js` `select()` redraws the inspector (root keeps `id="step-<i>"`). `ui/flow-problems.js` finds fields in the inspector, and a new `markProblems` sets the marks on the list rows. The inline styles of `ui/editor.js` are classes in `ui/css/pages/editor.css`.
  - **Docs and tests.** `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("The editor"). New `tests/ui-editor.test.ts`; `tests/ui-step-types.test.ts`, `tests/ui-flow-page.test.ts`, `tests/ui-flow-problems.test.ts` and `tests/ui-editor-styles.test.ts` are extended.
- UI states 4a — Runs list and My runs use the poller and the live states (#433). `renderRunsList` (`ui/runs.js`) and `renderMyRuns` (`ui/user/runs.js`) no longer use `setInterval`; they use `poller` every 30 s (`REFRESH_MS`). The admin list sets `hold: fieldFocused || dialogOpen`, so a refresh asks while the owner filter has focus but draws after it leaves. Unchanged data does not redraw.
  - **States.** Skeleton (`shape: "table"` / `"cards"`) on first load; one "Could not refresh. Showing data from …" note with Retry on a failed refresh; `errorState` with Retry on a first-load failure; `permissionState` (no Retry) on a 403. Both functions still return a cleanup and never reject. Empty: "No runs yet." with Start work (`#/start`); `filterEmpty` stays. Partial: when the queue or owners call fails, the runs are drawn with an inline note and Retry, and the queue card or owner filter is left out. The read-only preview shows no Remove button in any state.
  - **Code.** New `ui/run-states.js`: `NO_RUNS`, `noRuns()`, `part()` (a failed call as plain data, so the poller compares it), `partNote()` and `runsLiveStates()` (`liveStates` plus the 403 branch). A request counter drops older answers, also after an owner change or a Remove. The owner-filter rollback and the Remove dialog guard are kept.
  - **Docs and tests.** Runs row in `docs/ui-redesign/states.md`, My runs in `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`, line references in `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md`. New `tests/ui-runs-list-states.test.ts` and `tests/ui-user-runs-list-states.test.ts`; `tests/ui-a11y-live.test.ts` and the browser performance budget (`tests/browser/perf.ts`, `performance.spec.ts`) are updated for the poller.
- Skill experience 3a — flow syntax for skill modes (#437). A flow can set `skills: {mode: planned | explicit | off, ids}` and a claude step can set `skills: catalog | selected | off`. `explicit` locks the named ids at the first agent step under the same `skills.unresolved` policy; `off` gives no check, lock or skill block (it also skips `skills.selection.include`); step `off` gives no skill block and starts a new session. `recorded` and `catalog` are reserved. Flows without the fields run as before.

- Run detail 4c — Overview, Steps, Changes, Evidence and Logs tabs that keep their state (#393). New `ui/run-tabs.js` replaces the three-button tab strip of both run pages (`renderRunDetail` in `ui/runs.js`, `renderMyRun` in `ui/user/runs.js`; their own `showTab`, `tabButtons` and `tabSeq` are gone).
  - **Tabs.** Overview, Steps, Changes, Evidence, Logs, in that order (`data-tab` ids `overview`, `steps`, `diff`, `evidence`, `log`). The strip is a `role="tablist"` with `role="tab"` buttons, `aria-selected` and Left/Right arrow keys; each panel is a `role="tabpanel"`, hidden with the `hidden` attribute. A panel is built on first show and then only hidden or shown; a stream update never rebuilds an unaffected panel and never changes the tab. The default is Overview; a failed run opens on Steps once, unless the reader already picked a tab. The selected tab lives in memory for the life of the page. Addresses are unchanged.
  - **Panels.** Overview: the full task, the questions of a stopped run, the details (run id, flow and version, and for an administrator the workspace) and a step count ("3 of 5 steps done, 1 failed"); on the user page the answer form and "Answers given" are here, and typed text, caret and focus survive an update and a tab switch. Changes loads the diff on first open, on its own **Refresh** button and when the run finishes; open files and scroll stay. Evidence shows only what the run already has (no new API call): questions and answers with their time, the result of each finished shell or approval step, the next-step link, and "Already tried" of a failed run; with nothing, "No evidence recorded yet." Logs is the full live log for an administrator (step transcripts stay in Steps) and the filtered log for a user, who has no transcript anywhere. A run without steps shows "No steps recorded."
  - **Code.** `ui/run-tabs.js` exports `createTabs`, `overviewPanel`, `evidencePanel`, `evidenceItems` and `stepCountText` (plus small helpers). `ui/run-output.js` is adjusted for the shared tabs. No server change.
  - **Docs and tests.** `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` (the run page), `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md` line numbers. New `tests/ui-run-tabs.test.ts`; the `ui-next`, `ui-user-runs`, `ui-run-output`, `ui-a11y-live`, `ui-a11y-equivalents` and `ui-inline-styles` tests are updated.
- UI states 6 — Refinement (#347). The list and the session use the standard states. New `ui/refinement-states.js` (`failState`, `announcer`, `saveStates`, `isSplitConflict`, `splitConflict`, `issueLink`, `lostCard`) so `ui/refinement-draft.js` does not grow.
  - **Load.** Skeleton on first load; `errorState` with Retry (text from `errorText`); 403 is `permissionState`, 404 its own state with no Retry.
  - **Poll.** No redraw for unchanged data, `staleNote` with Retry on failure, and "The architect is working" as an inline line announced once. A poll never replaces a field with focus or unsaved text.
  - **Save.** Saving, saved and failed per field; a failed save keeps the text and offers Retry. `stay` in `ui/api.js` is unchanged.
  - **Publish.** A partial publish (some issues created, some failed) is a warning banner that lists both, with Retry for the rest; it stays until resolved. The success toast stays.
  - **Conflict.** A 409 "this draft is split…" shows a conflict state with a link to each part.
  - **Dialogs.** The eight native `confirm(` calls in `ui/refinement*.js` now use `confirmDialog`; none is left.
  - **Docs and tests.** Refinement row in `docs/ui-redesign/states.md`; `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md`; `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`. New `tests/ui-refinement-states.test.ts` and `tests/helpers/confirm-dialog.ts`; the `tests/ui-refinement*.test.ts` files are extended.
- UI states 4d — Start work: skeleton, empty states with the next step, start failure explained (#436). `renderStart` in `ui/user/start.js` (both displays) draws the heading and a `loadingState` first, then loads; it never rejects. A failing flows call shows `errorState(explainError(…))` with Retry, a 403 shows `permissionState`. No flows is an empty state: `NO_FLOWS_ADMIN` with a link to the flow editor for an admin, `NO_FLOWS` for a user. No repositories is an empty state with Add repository (`NO_REPOS`); a failing repository list shows an inline error with Retry in step 2 only, and other flows stay usable. A failed Start is explained by the new `explainStart` (no answer or a 5xx says the run may exist) in an alert under the form; the task and all fields keep their values, Start is enabled again and a second Start sends the same body. The field messages of `startProblem` are unchanged. The read-only preview shows no Start and no Add repository in any state. New exports `FLOWS_FAILED`, `FLOWS_DENIED`, `REPOS_FAILED`, `REPOS_FAILED_SAFE`, `START_FAILED`, `START_KEPT`, `START_UNSURE`, `START_UNSURE_NEXT`. `ui/graph.js` is unchanged. Docs: Start work row in `docs/ui-redesign/states.md`, `docs/ui-redesign/inventory.md`, `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`. Tests: new `tests/ui-user-start-states.test.ts`; `tests/ui-user-start.test.ts` and the CSS baseline are updated.
- Skill runtime 2 — carry the plan's skill request from `issue-plan` into `issue-code-daily` (#177, #416). New shell step `plan_check` after `daily_branch` reads the plan comments again (`gh issue view … | plan-comment`) and checks them against the Foundry's plan record. A matching record sets `run.skillCarry` (request, `planHash`, `lockHash`, `changes`); the run's skill lock is built from it at the first agent step, never from the comment text.
  - **Stops and warnings.** A deleted or edited plan comment, a newer plan without a record, a cleaned-up record or unreadable comments stop the run with `skills not resolved: … plan the issue again` when skills are in play (`skillPlan.action = "stop"`, no `skillCarry`; a resume runs `plan_check` again); with no skills they warn and go on. An invalid store always stops. Nothing stored means no carry and no stop. A record of another repository is never used.
  - **Changes.** Later human comments, changed `path:` evidence files or a changed technology hash carry with `changes` set and one warning each; `lockHash` then differs from `planHash` and an existing lock is made again. A plan gate in the run's history wins over a carry.
  - **Code.** `carryRunSkills` in `src/engine/plan-carry.ts` handles `plan_check` (exports `PLAN_CHECK_STEP` from `src/skills/request.ts`, `RunSkillCarry`); `check` in `src/skills/run-plan.ts` and `ensure` in `src/engine/skill-lock.ts` use the carry; `RunSummary.skillCarry` in `state.ts`; `runner.ts` runs the carry first and re-runs `plan_check` on resume after a carry stop. Changed paths are checked with `git --literal-pathspecs diff --quiet`. `flows/issue-code-daily.yaml` is regenerated from `scripts/build-flows.mjs`.
  - **Docs and tests.** `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("Skills from the plan to the coding run"). New `tests/skill-plan-check.test.ts`; the skill lock, skill gate run, pipeline and board tests are extended.
- Starter skill 5 — REST and OpenAPI interfaces (#190). New built-in skill `skills/rest-openapi/` (`skill.yaml`, `SKILL.md`, `REVIEW.md`; version 1.0.0, category `interface`, roles `coder` and `reviewer`, risk `low`). It applies to OpenAPI documents, HTTP routes and controllers, API clients, or an explicit plan to change a REST contract, and not to generic internal service changes or a passing HTTP mention. The coder instructions cover resources, methods, status codes, validation, error envelopes, pagination, idempotency, compatibility, versioning and security boundaries, stay language and framework neutral, and require the contract and the implementation to change together. The reviewer section checks backwards compatibility and failure responses. No code changes; the server pins the built-in at start like the other built-in skills. New `tests/skill-starter-rest-openapi.test.ts` covers schema, budgets, selection (server, client, contract-only and unrelated-mention fixtures), integrity, and behaviour evaluations.
- Starter skill 10 — new built-in skill `integration-testing` (#195). `skills/integration-testing/` (`skill.yaml`, `SKILL.md`, `REVIEW.md`; category `quality`, roles `coder` and `reviewer`). It tells a coding agent to add focused integration tests only when a change crosses a process, protocol, messaging or database boundary (not for unit-only changes), and separates unit, component, contract and integration tests. It prefers the repository's own test setup and uses Testcontainers only when already present or approved. It forbids arbitrary sleeps, shared mutable test state, disabled tests and production credentials. `REVIEW.md` gives reviewers checks for determinism, cleanup, failure paths and meaningful assertions. Fixtures cover HTTP, Kafka, database and unit-only tasks. No code or flow change. New `tests/skill-starter-integration-testing.test.ts` (schema, integrity, selection and behaviour checks).
- Starter skill 3 — TypeScript language engineering (#188). New built-in skill `skills/typescript` (`skill.yaml`, `SKILL.md`, `REVIEW.md`) for coder and reviewer: strict types, no broad `any`, unsafe assertions or ignored compiler errors without a documented exception, narrowing, generics, public and generated types, errors, promises and cancellation, module boundaries, type-level tests, the repository's own formatter, linter, package manager and compiler settings, and runtime validation at untyped boundaries. Candidates: `tsconfig.json` is now a `typescript` manifest in the repository profile and gives a new `typescript-config` signal (medium, 30; needs TypeScript sources in the module itself). A `typescript` dependency or tsconfig alone no longer makes a JavaScript-only module a candidate (the module or one below it needs TypeScript sources), and work whose affected files are all JavaScript (`.js`, `.jsx`, `.mjs`, `.cjs`) gets no TypeScript candidate unless the task names TypeScript or `tsconfig`. No Node.js or UI framework is assumed. New `tests/skill-typescript.test.ts` (browser, library and mixed JavaScript/TypeScript fixtures); `tests/skill-candidates.test.ts`, `tests/skill-registry.test.ts` and `tests/repo-profile.test.ts` are extended.
- Flow editor 2 — editor shell: state bar, one primary action, flow navigator, graph as an overview (#359). The flow page moves out of `ui/app.js` into `ui/flow-page.js` (`createFlowPage({ main, sidebar, state, api, onNavigate })`); `app.js` keeps `S`, `route()`, `welcome()` and the `beforeunload` and Ctrl/Cmd+S listeners. New `ui/flow-state.js` (pure `editorState(cur, flows)`, `filterFlows`, `overviewStore` that works when storage throws) and `ui/flow-shell.js` (header and navigator drawing).
  - **Header.** In Visual and YAML mode it shows the flow name and scope (`builtin`, `repo`, `global` or "not saved yet"), "Saved" or "Unsaved changes", a validation button ("Valid", "n problems", "Checking…") that moves focus to the problem list, and the save target in words ("Saves to this repo"; for a built-in flow "Saves your own copy to this repo").
  - **Actions.** One primary button: **Save** when the flow is unsaved or changed, otherwise **Test run** (the old ▶ Run; `runDialog` is unchanged). **Ask Claude**, **Save to** and **Delete flow** are in a "More" menu (`<details>`). The Visual/YAML switch stays visible.
  - **Navigator and overview.** The left list has a search box (name and description); the open flow stays listed. The graph is an "Overview" panel opened by a button; the choice is kept in `localStorage` (own key at 1100 px and below). At 1100 px and below it starts closed and opens above the form. `ui/style.css`: `.editor`, `.graph-pane` and `.dirty-dot` are replaced.
  - **Docs and tests.** `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md` and `inventory.md`, `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("The editor"), `docs/DESIGN.md`. The "flows" shot now expects "Test run"; `docs/images/flows.png` is stale until the screenshots are regenerated. New `tests/ui-flow-page.test.ts`.

- UI states 3d — Admin Home: one redraw, independent feeds and stale Problems (#426). `renderAdminHome` in `ui/home-admin.js` uses `poller` (every 30 s) and `liveStates` for Active (`focus: "home-active"`); `load`, `seq`, `loadFailed` and the interval are gone. The embedded Your turn gets `focus: "home-needs"`, so two failing feeds have different Retry names. The loader returns `{ runs, pending, health: { ok, value } }`: when only health fails, Problems keep the last good answer and show one `role="status"` line ("Problems could not be refreshed. Showing the last answer.", or "Problems could not be loaded." when there never was one); repeated failures do not re-mount it, the next good answer removes it, and the "Updated" note of Active is hidden while it shows. Every redraw goes through `redraw = () => keepScroll(box, …)`, so scroll and focus stay when a Your turn answer changes the page. A Your turn failure leaves Active working, and the other way round. Cleanup stops both pollers. Every Tab stop has a unique `data-focus` (`home-problem-<key>`, `home-findings`, `home-metrics`, `home-all-metrics`). Docs: Home row in `docs/ui-redesign/states.md`, `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`. Tests in `tests/ui-home-admin.test.ts`.
- Skill experience 1 — the run page shows the selected skills (#183). The run page and the user run page have a Skills card with Requested and Resolved skills: version, category, reason, evidence, context estimate, integrity (verified, changed, missing, …) and whether the skill was loaded, reloaded or reused in the session.
  - **API.** `GET /api/runs/:id` and the run event stream (`update`) carry a new `skillView`; the list and the queue do not. Users get no `digest`, `source` or `commit`, and no folder paths; admins do. Older runs have no `skillView`. Integrity and category come from the one-minute registry cache; `checkedAt` says when.
  - **Data.** `skillPlan.selected` entries also hold `selection`, `requiredBy`, `estimatedTokens` and `category`, so a run that stopped before locking still shows its selection. The lock stores each skill's `category`: a lock with categories is version 2, one without is version 1; both are read. A server rolled back to an older version rejects a version-2 lock and stops that run (fail closed); remove `skill-lock.json` by hand to recover. New `cachedSkillRegistryAt` in `src/skills/registry.ts`.
  - **Code.** New `src/server/skill-view.ts` (allowlist builder, never throws) and `ui/run-skills.js` (`skillsCard`), mounted in `ui/runs.js` and `ui/user/runs.js`. `UserRun` gets `skillView`.
  - **Docs and tests.** `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("Skills on the run page"). New `tests/skill-view.test.ts` and `tests/ui-run-skills.test.ts`; the unresolved, run-lock, user-view, run-ownership, ui-next, ui-user-runs and inline-styles tests are extended.
- Starter skill 1 — built-in `java` skill for Java language engineering (#186). New package `skills/java/` (`skill.yaml`, `SKILL.md`, `REVIEW.md`), version 1.0.0, category `language`, roles coder and reviewer. It says when it applies (Java sources, or a Maven/Gradle build that compiles Java) and when it does not (Kotlin-, Scala- or Groovy-only modules, non-Java work; framework and database rules belong to their own skills). It covers source and runtime version, API compatibility, null handling, exceptions, resource management, concurrency, collections, repository conventions and compact JUnit guidance, and prefers existing project conventions and dependencies over new ones. Budgets: planner description 160 characters, coder 1200 tokens, reviewer 400 tokens. The existing `SKILL_RULES` entry does the triggering; the manifest detector is declarative only. New `tests/skill-starter-java.test.ts` (manifest, description, budgets, plain Java, mixed JVM and non-Java fixtures through catalogue, integrity, resolve, payload and run evaluation); `tests/skill-registry.test.ts` checks that `java` loads as built-in. The behaviour checks do not run an agent. No code or flow change; as for every built-in skill, a later change to the package needs a new `version`.
- Fix: finished stories failed after waiting two hours for their turn to merge into `develop`, and every retry then failed at the first step. (1) The run that holds the merge turn starts its tests at once instead of queueing for a test slot behind other stories. (2) A run waits up to 8 hours for its turn instead of 2. (3) A retry of a story whose work an earlier run finished and committed goes straight to the merge (conflicts are resolved there) instead of failing or building it again.
- Skill runtime 4b — Codex steps are isolated from the personal Codex setup (#421). With `isolate_agents: true`, a Codex step with a local model or a `CODEX_API_KEY` it really sees uses a private Codex folder, `<run folder>/home/.codex` (record `codexHome: "run"`); `OPENAI_API_KEY` alone does not select it. A step with a Codex login gets `--ignore-user-config`, so `config.toml` and its MCP servers are skipped and the login keeps working. `--ignore-rules` is never passed. Isolated steps get an agent note in `<instructions>` (`CODEX_AGENT_NOTE`) and a `Codex:` log line. `codexExecFlags()` reads `codex exec --help` (or `exec resume --help`) and caches it for 10 minutes; a CLI without the flag gets no flag and a `!` log line, and the step still runs. A private folder that cannot be made refuses the step; it never falls back to the personal folder. A step that moves between the personal and the private folder does not resume (#420). Boxed steps and `isolate_agents: false` are unchanged. New in `src/agents/codex-home.ts`: `codexIsolationMode`, `privateCodexHome`, `codexIsolationLine`, `CODEX_HOME_REFUSED`. `fake-codex.mjs` handles `--help` and `SHOWCODEXHOME`. New `tests/codex-isolation-flow.test.ts` and `tests/codex-isolation-refusal.test.ts`; `tests/agents.test.ts` and `tests/codex-home.test.ts` are extended.
  - **Private folder and HOME.** `privateCodexHome` also returns `HOME=<run folder>/home`, so personal skills in `~/.agents/skills` are out of reach too.
  - **Reviewer.** A Codex reviewer step keeps its MCP and hook overrides in every mode. `--ignore-user-config` is passed once in login mode and not at all in the private, unsupported and boxed cases. With `isolate_agents: false` the reviewer keeps the flag as before, with no note and no `Codex:` line.
  - **Upgrade.** With `isolate_agents: true`, Codex steps no longer read `~/.codex/config.toml`. With a key or a local model they use a private folder, where personal command rules do not apply either. `isolate_agents: false` restores the old behaviour.
- UI states 3b — the Board refreshes through the poller, with filter buttons that keep focus (#424). `renderBoard` in `ui/board.js` uses `poller` and `liveStates` from `ui/live.js`; its own timer, busy and visibility code is gone, and it still returns the cleanup at once. First load shows a skeleton under the heading; a first-load failure shows `errorState` with Retry. A failed refresh keeps the cards and shows "Could not refresh. Showing data from …" with Retry once; the next success removes it. "Updated HH:MM" is shown. A hidden tab makes no requests; on return the tick of a watcher whose GitHub link was opened is sent first, then one GET. Page scroll and the sideways scroll of `.board` (`data-scroll="board"`) are kept across a redraw, and a redraw waits while a select has focus. `filterBar` and `filterEmpty` in `ui/filters.js` take `{ focus = "filter" }` and name their buttons `${focus}-remove-${key}`, `${focus}-clear` and `${focus}-empty-clear`, so every Board Tab stop has a unique `data-focus`; the Runs lists keep the default names. Docs: Board row in `docs/ui-redesign/states.md`, `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("Pages that refresh themselves"). New `tests/ui-board-live.test.ts`; `tests/ui-board.test.ts`, `tests/ui-filters.test.ts` and `tests/ui-a11y-live.test.ts` are updated.
- UI states 3c — Your turn and the user Home refresh through the poller (#425). `ui/turn.js` and `ui/home.js` use `poller` and `liveStates` from `ui/live.js`.
  - **Behaviour.** Both pages show a skeleton before the first answer, keep the scroll position, make no requests in a hidden tab and redraw only when the data changed. A failed refresh keeps the items and shows one banner with Retry. A first-load failure shows `errorState` ("Could not load what needs you." / "Home could not be loaded.") with Retry; `renderYourTurn` and `renderHome` no longer reject. A redraw waits while a dialog is open. The standalone Your turn page shows an "Updated" note; the embedded one does not. `startBadge` still asks every 30 s in a hidden tab, for the tab title.
  - **Code.** `ui/turn.js`: new internal `ask(call)` (the `gen` check, count and `openWhenWaiting`; no page draw); `refresh(call)` is `ask` then `page`; one poll gives one `onState` call. `renderYourTurn` takes a new option `focus`, passed to `liveStates`. The local `held`/`waiting`/`mine` code is gone. `ui/home.js`: `seq` and `gone` are gone; every Tab stop of `homeView` has a unique `data-focus` (`home-action`, `home-open-<id>`, `home-details-<id>`, `home-status-<id>`, `home-more-<slug>`, `home-summary-<slug>`, `home-ask-<i>`, `home-start`, `home-repos`).
  - **Docs and tests.** Home row in `docs/ui-redesign/states.md`. New `tests/ui-turn-live.test.ts`; `tests/ui-home.test.ts` and `tests/ui-a11y-live.test.ts` are extended.
- Skill experience 2 — selection, activation and context-budget evaluations (#184). An eval case can have `skills:` with `selected`, `absent`, `one_of`, `max_tokens` and `max_attached_tokens` (new `src/skills/eval.ts`, `evaluateSkillRun`). A result has `quality` (the old check) and `skills` (selection, context and activation verdicts); `passed` needs both, so a successful run can fail on wrong selection or on budget. The summary adds `qualityRate` and a `skills` block of rates. `scf eval` prints a second table; the Dashboard Evaluations card gets a "Skills" column. `runEval` takes `codexBin`. Suites without `skills` and older reports are unchanged. Not done yet: Codex and local-model end-to-end variants (waiting on #182) and recording every retry attempt. Docs: `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("Skill checks", promotion evidence). New `tests/skill-eval.test.ts`; `tests/evals.test.ts` and `tests/ui-dashboard.test.ts` are extended.
- UI states 7 — Build (#348). Flows, a flow, Library and Models show loading, empty, failed, partial and permission states. A failed save, delete or run is explained and keeps the draft. The five confirmations (discard, overwrite flow, delete flow, overwrite block, delete block) use `confirmDialog` instead of the browser dialog. The model test result stays after Reload. "Draft with Claude" and Run cannot be closed while they work. New `tests/helpers/ui-load.ts`, `tests/ui-flow-states.test.ts`, `tests/ui-library-states.test.ts`, `tests/ui-models-states.test.ts` and `tests/ui-flow-save.test.ts`. `docs/ui-redesign/states.md` and `dialogs.md` are updated.
- Run detail 4b — one stable summary header on both run pages (#392). New `ui/run-header.js` exports `createRunHeader`, `headerFacts`, `mainAction`, `runTitle`, `runTiming`, `answerable`, `RETRY_LABEL` and the moved `firstLine`, `workText` and `runActions` (still re-exported from `ui/user/runs.js`).
  - **Header.** Task title, status with "?", repository and issue or PR, branch, started and duration, "resumed N×". An administrator also sees owner, cost and "Open flow"; for a user these fields are never read. Nodes are built once and `update` changes only what differs, so a focused button keeps focus and an open "?" note stays open. Old runs without `history`, `next`, `flowDef` or `startedAt` show "—". The failure card or "What happens next", the retired-flow line, queue position and step row sit directly under it.
  - **Main button.** Approve or "Retry from the failing step" is filled; Cancel is never filled, and nothing is filled while the answer form shows. "Resume at …" and plain "Retry" are gone (D16). The admin step button reads "Retry from the failing step" (step in `title`); "Retry from step…" stays.
  - **Behaviour change.** Retry is offered only when the run has a step to continue at (`runActions`).
  - **Code and tests.** `ui/runs.js` uses the header and exports `money`; `renderMyRun` calls `header.update`; `timeAgo` in `ui/dom.js` takes an optional `now`. New `tests/ui-run-header.test.ts`; the `ui-next`, `ui-user-runs`, `ui-view-as`, `ui-a11y-live` and `server` tests are updated. `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md` line numbers and `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` are updated. No server change.

- Refinement 9e-3b — publish replaces a split issue: dependants rewritten and commented, the original commented (#418).
  - **When.** Once every leaf part is on GitHub after the creates of this publish (`replaceState` is `"due"`), `POST /api/refinement/:id/publish` replaces the original. Order: journal the dependants (`recordReplacing`); handle each unfinished dependant in issue-number order (read, record the evidence, read again, PATCH, comment, `recordDependantDone`); comment on the original last; `recordReplaced`. While a part is not ready only the ready parts are created, nothing is written to the original or its dependants, and the view has `replace: "waiting"`. A `published` session that is `"due"` (also an old one) is finished by publishing with `{}`.
  - **Dependants.** An issue with `#N` under "Depends on" gets one PATCH with `body` only (no `title`) and a comment with Before/After. An issue that names the original by title gets only a "change by hand" comment. Closed or gone issues get no write (outcomes `closed`, `gone`). Outcomes: `rewritten`, `by-hand`, `check`, `closed`, `gone`, `no-longer-depends`.
  - **Original.** It is not PATCHed and not closed. Its comment names the parts, says it stays open and should be checked and closed by hand, adds the 1,000-issue warning when `cut`, and lists the acceptance criteria left behind (one fixed sentence when the list is too long). The marker is the last line. Every trigger label of an issues watcher of the repository (enabled or not, matched without regard to case) is taken off the original first, so the Foundry does not also build it. The labels are kept in `replacing.labels` (at most 100 are named; more are still removed) so a retry names them and removes nothing twice; the comment names them. `source.buildLabel` is always dropped when the replacement ends.
  - **Answer.** `replaced: { issue, parts, dependants: [{ issue, outcome }] }`, also when nothing was created. The session then has `replacedBy`, `closed: "open"` and no `replacing`; the view has `replace: "done"`. `planOf` and both answers no longer give `notChanged` when `replaces` is set.
  - **Checks before the first write** (when the publish creates a part or is due): original closed and replacement not started: 409, nothing written. Original being built and replacement not started: 409, "nothing was written". Started and being built: 409 without "nothing was written", no comment on the original. Started and closed: 200, `closed: "other"`, and the comment says the issue was closed already. A publish that creates no part and is not due does not read the original.
  - **Concurrent edits.** An edit between the two reads outside the "Depends on" text is kept. An edit inside it gives 409 with no PATCH of that issue; publishing again rewrites it and replaces the stored evidence.
  - **Retry safety.** After a PATCH whose comment failed, the retry sends no second PATCH and the comment has the stored Before/After. An own comment with the marker is not added twice; one from another login does not count. A stored write whose range holds neither the reference nor the stored `rangeAfter` gets the "check" comment. An entry no longer found is still finished; a dependant that appears between two tries is handled. A session file that cannot be saved gives 500 naming the last write and "publish again"; the retry finishes without a second PATCH or comment.
  - **Own comment.** `findOwnComment` in `src/github.ts` is shared with `updateOne`. With a GitHub App sign-in and no `viewerDidAuthor` it fails with `APP_NO_AUTHOR` (502) and does not call `api user`; `updateOne` keeps its 502 message.
  - **Ledger.** One ledger holds every write of a publish (created and updated drafts, PATCHes, comments, labels). Errors say "nothing was written" only when it is empty, "was changed" only for a PATCH and "got a comment" for a comment. `auditLines` (`src/server/api-refinement-replace.ts`) builds the audit lines: at most 500 characters each, `(i/n)` at the end when there is more than one, every number once.
  - **Session rules.** Importing the original while a session of it is `"due"` gives 409 `duplicate` with the session id. Removing the split mark draft is refused (409) once a part is on GitHub. Restoring a dropped `"due"` session is refused when another open session of the issue exists (`openOfIssue` counts it). A dropped session blocks nothing.
  - **Known limit.** GitHub has no conditional update, so an edit made between the last read of a dependant and its PATCH can be lost. Old `published` sessions with a split original count as not replaced yet and finish with a publish of `{}`.
  - **Safety.** No `gh` command line holds issue text; text goes through stdin only.
  - **Code.** `withWrite(…, again)` in `replace-journal.ts`; `recordDependantWrite(…, ev, opts, again?)` in `store.ts`; `originalComment` in `dependants.ts`; `checkOriginal`, `hasOwnComment`, `replaceOriginal` and `auditLines` in `api-refinement-replace.ts`. Permission description and the `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` table row updated. Tests: new `tests/refinement-publish-replace-api.test.ts` and `tests/refinement-publish-replace-retry-api.test.ts`, plus unit tests; the fake `gh` and `hold(on, name)` in `tests/helpers/fake-github.ts` can hold on two files.
  - **Known limit.** GitHub has no conditional update, so an edit made between the last read and the PATCH can be lost.
  - **Compatibility.** Old published sessions with a split original count as not replaced yet; publishing them with `{}` finishes the replacement.
- UI states 3a — `ui/live.js`: one poller, scroll keeping and live states (#423). New exports `poller`, `keepScroll`, `liveStates` and `dialogOpen`. The poller sends one request at a time, only in a visible tab and not offline (the offline signal is ignored on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1` and `[::1]`), awaits `wake()` on return to the tab, and draws only when the data changed. `ui/states.js`: new `staleText(at, failed)` (used by `staleNote`); `errorState` takes `{ focus }` and a `banner` action takes `focus` (sets `data-focus`). Existing callers behave as before. `FakeElement` in `tests/helpers/fake-dom.ts` gets `scrollTop` and `scrollLeft`. New `tests/ui-live.test.ts`; `tests/ui-states.test.ts` is extended. `docs/ui-redesign/states.md` has the section "Background refresh (part 3, #344)". No page uses it yet.
- UI states 3a — `ui/live.js`: one poller, scroll keeping and live states (#423). New exports `poller`, `keepScroll`, `liveStates` and `dialogOpen`. The poller sends one request at a time, only in a visible tab and not offline (the offline signal is ignored on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1` and `[::1]`), awaits `wake()` on return to the tab, and draws only when the data changed. `ui/states.js`: new `staleText(at, failed)` (used by `staleNote`); `errorState` takes `{ focus }` and a `banner` action takes `focus` (sets `data-focus`). Existing callers behave as before. `FakeElement` in `tests/helpers/fake-dom.ts` gets `scrollTop` and `scrollLeft`. New `tests/ui-live.test.ts`; `tests/ui-states.test.ts` is extended. `docs/ui-redesign/states.md` has the section "Background refresh (part 3, #344)". Pages adopt it part by part.

- UI states 3e — health line (#427). `ui/health.js` refreshes through `poller`: one request at a time, none in a hidden tab (one when you return), no redraw for an unchanged answer. "The Foundry server does not answer" is drawn once with `role="alert"`. **Cancel run** asks in `confirmDialog` ("Cancel this run?" / "Keep running" / "Cancel the run") instead of the native confirm; the line is not redrawn while a dialog is open. Docs: `dialogs.md`, `states.md`, `USER_GUIDE.md`. Tests: `tests/ui-health.test.ts`, `tests/ui-a11y-live.test.ts`.

- UI states 5 — Repositories (#346). `ui/repos.js`, `ui/admin-repos.js`, `ui/admin-credentials.js` and `ui/watcher-form.js` use the standard states.
  - **Loading and failure.** My repositories, All repositories and Credentials draw a skeleton table on first load. A failed load shows `errorState` with Retry (the server text from `plainError`, wrapped by `explainError`), or `permissionState` on a 403. A failed `repoMethods` call is partial: the list shows with a warning banner (with Retry) that adding is not possible now.
  - **Stale.** A reload after an action keeps the list on screen and shows `staleNote` with Retry on failure, instead of an error toast only. Only the newest fetch of a page draws.
  - **Provider failure.** A failed connection test stays on the row ("Connection failed" with the reason; a test that could not run shows its error) until the next test; the button reads "Test again". Secrets never appear in these texts.
  - **Success.** "Repository added" stays a toast. For a deploy-key repository whose connection does not work yet, a banner on the page asks for the deploy key on GitHub until it works.
  - **Dialogs.** Remove repository and Generate a new key use `confirmDialog` with the same texts as before; no native `confirm(` is left in `ui/repos.js`. Read-only preview: no action buttons in any state.
  - **Code.** New exports in `ui/repos.js`: `connectionProblem`, `explainRepoError`, `loadFailed`, `needsDeployKey`. The watcher form links to My repositories when there are no repositories.
  - **Docs and tests.** `docs/ui-redesign/dialogs.md`, the Repositories row of the matrix in `docs/ui-redesign/states.md`, `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` ("Repository pages"). New `tests/ui-repos-states.test.ts`; the repos, admin-repos, admin-credentials, watchers and a11y-admin tests are extended.
- UI states 8 — Administration (#349). Users, Watchers, Problems, Dashboard, Audit and Settings show a skeleton on first load and an `errorState` with Retry on failure. Dashboard: each failed call of the six (`partNote`, e.g. "Could not load evals.") is named in its own section and the rest is shown. Watchers: "Checking…" is an inline busy state next to the button; a failed monitor call shows an error in the monitor card only. Users, Watchers and Problems keep their list when a reload fails and show `staleNote` with Retry; the newest reload wins. Settings: "Saving…", "Saved at …" and "Not saved." next to Save, plus the toast. Delete watcher, Clean up workspaces and the unreadable state file use `confirmDialog` (`confirmUnreadable` in `ui/monitor.js`); no native `confirm(` is left in `ui/admin.js` and `ui/problems.js`. Audit keeps its `my === loads` guard and uses the shared empty and error states. Dashboard, Watchers, Problems and Settings draw into their own box in `ui/app.js`, so a late answer cannot touch the next page. Docs: Administration row of `docs/ui-redesign/states.md`, `inventory.md`, `dialogs.md`, `docs/USER_GUIDE.md`. New `tests/ui-users-states.test.ts`, `tests/ui-watchers-states.test.ts`, `tests/ui-settings-states.test.ts`; the audit, dashboard, problems and monitor tests are extended.
- Skill runtime 4a — Record the Codex folder of a session and resume only in the same folder (#420).
  - **Record.** Every Codex step records its Codex folder: `run` for the run's own folder, or `personal:` plus 12 hex digits. The value never holds a path. It is `codexHome` on the step record, `steps.<id>.codex_home` in templates, and it comes back from `run.json` on a resume of the run.
  - **Resume.** A Codex step with `resume:` starts a new session, and logs why, when the recorded folder differs from its own or none was recorded. With the same folder it resumes as before. Claude steps are unchanged.
  - **Tests.** The test setup removes an inherited `CODEX_API_KEY` and `CODEX_HOME`.
  - **Upgrade.** A run started before the upgrade starts a new Codex session once where a step would have resumed one.
- Skill runtime 2b — `issue-plan` writes a plan record, and `scf clean --purge` removes old ones (#415). `post_plan` now builds the comment in a variable, hashes it with `plan-comment hash` before posting, and prints the `gh` link, a `PLAN_COMMENT_SHA256: sha256:<hex>` line and the `SKILL_REQUEST:` line last. A failing hash tool or `gh` fails the step; with the tool missing, nothing is posted. New `src/engine/plan-carry.ts` (`carryRunSkills`) writes the record after `post_plan` (request, comment ID, comment hash, HEAD commit, technology hash), only when the step calls `plan-comment`; custom flows without it behave as before. If the record cannot be written, the run stops with `skills not resolved: …` and resumes at `post_plan` when skills are in play, otherwise it only logs a warning. New `stopRunSkills` in `src/skills/run-plan.ts`; `headCommit` is exported from `src/engine/skill-lock.ts`. `scf clean --purge` and the Admin clean-up remove plan records older than the larger of 90 days and the cut-off, and report `and N plan record(s)` (a dry run only counts). Records are only touched for the real data folder (or `planRecordsHome`); a locked or unwritable store is reported. `flows/issue-plan.yaml` is regenerated.
- UI redesign navigation 3b — repository filter on Refinement, My repositories and All repositories (#351). `#/refinement?repo=…`, `#/repos?repo=…` and `#/all-repos?repo=…` show only the matching sessions or records, with the filter bar of #350 (remove and **Clear filters**). The **New refinement session** dialog preselects the repository when it is in the list. A repository that is not in the answer shows "This repository is not in your list. It may have been removed, or you may not have access." with **Clear filters**, the same text in every case. `#/refinement/<id>` ignores the query. The `onPage()` guards use `splitHash(location.hash).path`, so a query does not break them, and `reload()` keeps the filter. `ui/filters.js` gets `githubOf(url)` (`https://github.com/o/n(.git)` and `git@github.com:o/n(.git)` → `owner/name`), `matchesRepo`, `filterGone` and `NOT_IN_LIST`. No server change. Tests extended in `tests/ui-refinement.test.ts`, `tests/ui-repos.test.ts`, `tests/ui-admin-repos.test.ts` and `tests/ui-filters.test.ts`. The user guide section "Filters in the address" is extended.
- Component kit 6 — record components: evidence, timeline entries, logs and diffs (#330). New `ui/kit/records.js` and `ui/kit/records.css` export `evidence`, `timelineEntry`, `timeline`, `logView` and `diffView`. `logView` is a `role="log"` region with a name and `tabindex="0"`, coloured by tokens so it follows the theme. `diffView` takes the `{ stat, patch }` data of `api.diff`, marks file, hunk, added and removed lines by class, keeps the `+` and `-` characters and shows the "Diff truncated" notice when `truncated` is set. Neither cuts its data. All text is added as text nodes. Every component draws with missing data (no time, steps or lines). The gallery has sections for long lines, 500 log lines, a large diff, empty states and the narrow layout. `docs/UI_COMPONENTS.md` has the five entries and the "Building a new screen" checklist. New `tests/ui-kit-records.test.ts`; `tests/ui-gallery.test.ts` and `tests/ui-kit-actions.test.ts` are extended. No existing page is changed.
- Skill runtime 2a — plan record store and the `plan-comment` tool (#414). New `src/skills/plan-record.ts` (store under `<data folder>/skill-plans/`, own lock `skill-plans.lock`, 20 records per issue, age clean-up with a `purged.json` note) and `src/skills/plan-record-rules.ts` (`pickPlanRecord`, `planChanges`, `technologyHashOf`, the parsers). New `tools/plan-comment`: hashes a comment body, and lists the plan comments of an issue as IDs and hashes, never text. `projectEvidence` is exported from `src/skills/run-lock.ts`. Nothing calls them yet; no behaviour change.
- Refinement 9e-3a — the publish plan shows how a split issue will be replaced (#417). Nothing is written; no UI change.
  - `GET /api/refinement/:id/publish` has `replaces: { issue, parts, ready, cut?, dependants }`, with `before`/`after` or `byHand` for each open issue that depends on the original.
  - `GET /api/refinement/:id` has `source.replace` (`waiting`, `due`, `done`) and no longer shows `source.replacing`.
  - New server option `openIssuePages` (default 10).
  - `mergeJournal` keeps an unfinished entry that the scan no longer finds.
- Skill runtime 5 — reuse skills across repairs and reload them on fallback (#180).
  - **Behaviour.** A Claude step with `resume:` that continues a session holding the same locked block gets a one-line reminder instead of the full skill text. A new session (retry, fallback to another model or provider, or a resume that cannot continue) gets the complete block once. The lock is still verified before every session; a changed registry stops the step.
  - **Logs and records.** One line per session: `skill context: reused|loaded|reloaded …` with ids and sizes only, and `! skill context: rejected (…)`. The step record `skills` gains `state`, `digest`, `attachedBytes` and `attachedEstimatedTokens`; the step record gains `skills_digest`. Older run files stay valid.
  - **Code.** `attachSkillPayload`, `skillPayloadDigest`, `skillReminder` in `src/skills/payload.ts`; `sessionToResume` in `src/agents/run.ts`; the load log line moved from `skillSession` to the runner. The agent note says the `<foundry-skills>` block may come from an earlier prompt of the session.
  - **Docs.** `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` "Skills in Claude sessions".
  - **Tests.** `tests/skill-payload.test.ts`, `tests/agents.test.ts`, `tests/engine-skill-lock.test.ts`, `tests/skill-run-lock-flow.test.ts`.

- Skill runtime 6 — compact review checks from selected skills for reviewers (#181).
  - **Package.** A skill may hold an optional `REVIEW.md` (at most 8 KiB, not empty, needs the `reviewer` role or no roles). It is part of the digest. New `SkillPackage.review` and `SKILL_LIMITS.reviewMdBytes`.
  - **Flow.** New optional `skill_role: coder | reviewer` on `claude` steps. A reviewer step must be read-only, allow only `Read`, `Glob`, `Grep` and `LS`, and not `resume` (`reviewerStepProblem`, `readOnlyStep` in `src/flow/schema.ts`); checked at load and again before the step runs. `plan_review` and the `review_N` steps of `issue-plan` are reviewers (`scripts/build-flows.mjs`; `flows/*.yaml` regenerated).
  - **Context.** `renderReviewPayload` and `skillSession(…, role)` build a `<foundry-skills role="reviewer">` block with description and `REVIEW.md` only, from skills loaded for coding that allow the reviewer role. Plan review uses the draft plan's request (`draftSkillRequest`). The block is smaller than the coding block and limited by new `skills.review.max_tokens` (default 3000) and `max_skill_tokens` (default 1000).
  - **Agents.** Claude and Codex reviewers get the same block. A reviewer Claude session runs without the personal setup; Codex reviewers run with `--ignore-user-config`, no MCP servers and no hooks.
  - **Compatibility.** Steps without `skill_role` and runs without skills behave as before. The step record's `skills` gains an optional `role`.
  - **Docs.** `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` "Review checks for reviewers"; `docs/FLOW_AUTHORING.md` `skill_role`.
  - **Tests.** New `tests/skill-review.test.ts`; `tests/skill-registry.test.ts`.
- Component kit 5 — product components (#329).
